function\n\n\tprivate function checkFloat(fldName, id)\n\t\tcheckFloat = true\n\t\tdim val : val = m_Request.Form(\"\" & id)\n\t\tif len(val) > 0 then\n\t\t\tif not isnumeric(val) id)\n\t\tcheckZip = true\n\t\tdim val : val = m_Request.Form(\"\" & id)\n\t\tval = replace(val,\" \", \"\")\n\t\tval = replace(val,\"-\", \"\")\n\n\t\tif len(val) > 0 then\n\t\t\t'check only if form       "source": "<%\n'**\n'* @file\n'*   String functions for the CMS Application.\n'*\n\n'**\n'* Repeat a string x times\n'*\nfunction strx(byval x, byval str)\n dim r,i\n r = str\n if isNumeric(x) and r <> \"\" then\n \tif x > 0 then\n\t\tfor i = 1 to x\n\t\t\tr = r & str\n\t\tnext\n\tend if\n end if\n strx = r\nend function\n\n'**\n'* Construct a string from the message and array of parameters to fill the\n'* message similar to syntax of ruby's build_message() function, similar to C's\n'* prints() function.\n'*\n'* @usage \\code\n'*   build_message(\"Hello <?>\", Array[\"world\"])\n'*   build_message(\"Hello <?>, its <?>!\", Array[\"world\", Year]) ' prints Hello world, its 1999!\n'* \\endcode\nfunction build_message(byval message, byref arr)\n\t'writeln \"build_message(message='\" & message & \"', arr=\" & dprint_r(arr)\n\tdim val\n\tfor each val in arr\n\t\tselect case varType(val)\n\t\t\tcase 0'Empty (uninitialized)\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[undefined]\"\n\t\t\tcase 1'Null (no valid data)\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[null]\"\n\t\t\tcase 2'Integer\n\t\t\tcase 3'Long integer\n\t\t\tcase 4,5'Single/Double precision floating-point number\n\t\t\t\tval = iif(val Mod 1 = 0, val & \".0\", val)\n\t\t\tcase 6'Currency\n\t\t\tcase 7'Date\n\t\t\tcase 8'String\n\t\t\tcase 9'Automation object\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[\" & iif(try(val, \".name\"), evaluate(val, \".name\"), TypeName(val)) & \" Object]\"\n\t\t\tcase 10'Error\n\t\t\t\tval = val.source\n\t\t\tcase 11'Boolean\n\t\t\tcase 12'Variant (used only with arrays of Variants)\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[Variant Array]\"\n\t\t\tcase 13'Data-access object\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[Data-access Object]\"\n\t\t\tcase 17'Byte\n\t\t\tcase 8192'Array\n\t\t\tcase 8204'Variant Array\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[Array]\"\n\t\t\tcase else\n\t\t\t\tval = \"[unknown type:\" & varType(val) & \"]\"\n\t\tend select\n\t\tmessage = replace(message, \"<\" & \"?\" & \">\", val, 1, 1)\n\tnext\n\tbuild_message = message\nend function\n\n\n'**\n'* This function takes a string and removes file extensions, converts\n'* underscores to spaces and un-does CamelCase.\n'*\n'* @param text (string)\n'*   The string to prettify.\n'* @return String\n'*   A prettified version of the provided text string.\nfunction PrettyText(text)\n\tdim anExtension : set anExtension = new RegExp\n\tdim a : a = cstr(text)\n\tanExtension.pattern = \"(\\.asp|\\.txt|\\.htm|\\.html|\\.inc|\\.jpg|\\.jpeg|\\.gif|\\.png|\\.zip|\\.gz|\\.tar)\"\n\tanExtension.global = true\n\tdim camel_case : set camel_case = new RegExp\n\tcamel_case.pattern = \"([a-z]?)([0-9]+)([A-Z])|([a-z])([A-Z])\"\n\tcamel_case.global = true\n\n\ta = replace(a, \"_\", \" \")\n\ta = anExtension.replace(a, \"\")\n\ta = camel_case.replace(a, \"$1$4 $2 $3$5\")\n\n\tset anExtension = nothing\n\tset camel_case = nothing\n\tPrettyText = a\nend function\n\n'**\n'* Convert a string into CamelCase (capitalize each word and remove spaces).\n'* This is useful for creating and referencing Anchor tags within a document\n'* based off of a regular Text heading.\n'*\nfunction CamelCase(byval text)\n\tif (not isnull(text) and (not text = \"\")) then\n\t\ttext = replace(pcase(text), \" \", \"\")\n\tend if\n\tCamelCase = text\nend function\n\n\n'*\n'* This function takes a string and converts to Proper Case.\n'* Prototyped by: Brian Shamblen on 3/18/99\n'* This version by: ASP 101 Sample Code - http://www.asp101.com/\n'*\nfunction PCase(text)\n\tdim iPosition  ' Our current position in the string (First character = 1)\n\tdim iSpace     ' The position of the next space after our iPosition\n\tdim result  ' Our temporary string used to build the function's output\n\n\t' Set our position variable to the start of the string.\n\tiPosition = 1\n\n\t' We loop through the string checking for spaces.\n\t' If there are unhandled spaces left, we handle them...\n\tdo while InStr(iPosition, text, \" \", 1) <> 0\n\t\t' To begin with, we find the position of the offending space.\n\t\tiSpace = InStr(iPosition, text, \" \", 1)\n\n\t\t' We uppercase (and append to our output) the first character after\n\t\t' the space which was handled by the previous run through the loop.\n\t\tresult = result & UCase(Mid(text, iPosition, 1))\n\n\t\t' We lowercase (and append to our output) the rest of the string\n\t\t' up to and including the current space.\n\t\tresult = result & LCase(Mid(text, iPosition + 1, iSpace - iPosition))\n\n\t\t' Note:\n\t\t' The above line is something you may wish to change to not convert\n\t\t' everything to lowercase.  Currently things like \"McCarthy\" end up\n\t\t' as \"Mccarthy\", but if you do change it, it won't fix things like\n\t\t' ALL CAPS.  I don't see an easy compromise so I simply did it the\n\t\t' way I'd expect it to work and the way the VB command\n\t\t' StrConv(string, vbProperCase) works.  Any other functionality is\n\t\t' left \"as an exercise for the reader!\"\n\n\t\t' Set our location to start looking for spaces to the\n\t\t' position immediately after the last space.\n\t\tiPosition = iSpace + 1\n\tloop\n\n\t' Because we loop until there are no more spaces, it leaves us\n\t' with the last word uncapitalized so we handle that here.\n\t' This also takes care of capitalizing single word strings.\n\t' It's the same as the two lines inside the loop except the\n\t' second line LCase's to the end and not to the next space.\n\tresult = result & UCase(Mid(text, iPosition, 1))\n\tresult = result & LCase(Mid(text, iPosition + 1))\n\n\t' That's it - Set our return value and exit\n\tPCase = result\nend function\n\n'**\n'* Convert a string of text to Proper Case (aka, Title Case), capitalizing the\n'* first letter of each word.\n'* by: <james@elementalidad.com> 10/18/07\nfunction scase(byval text)\n\tif not isNull(text) and len(text) > 0 then\n\t\tdim rgxSentence, m : set rgxSentence = new RegExp\n\t\trgxSentence.pattern = \"[.]\\s+?\\b([a-z])\"\n\t\trgxSentence.ignoreCase = true\n\t\trgxSentence.global = true\n\t\ttext = UCase(Mid(text, 1, 1)) & LCase(Mid(text, 2, len(text)))\n\t\tfor each m in rgxSentence.execute(text)\n\t\t\ttext = replace(text, m.value, replace(m.value, m.submatches(0), ucase(m.submatches(0))))\n\t\tnext\n\tend if\n\tscase = text\nend function\n\n'! @class FastString\n'!\n'! Fast String Class:  Use this when you want\n'! to concatenate strings (implemented by an\n'! internal array).\n'!\n'! Written by Marcus Tucker, July 2004\n'! http://marcustucker.com\n'!\nclass FastString\n\tprivate m_counter\n\tprivate m_array()\n\tprivate m_length\n\tprivate m_initial_length\n\n\t'**\n\t'* Constructor initializes the class and its variables.\n\tprivate sub class_initialize()\n\t\tm_counter = 0\n\t\tm_initial_length = 128\n\t\tredim m_array(m_initial_length - 1)\n\t\tm_length = m_initial_length\n\tend sub\n\n\t'**\n\t'* Destructor removes an instance from memory.\n\tprivate sub class_terminate()\n\t\terase m_array\n\tend sub\n\n\t'**\n\t'* Concatenation function adds a new string to the end of the existing one.\n\t'*\n\t'* @param text (string)\n\t'*   The text string to add.\n\tpublic sub add(byref text)\n\t\tm_array(m_counter) = text\n\t\tm_counter = m_counter + 1\n\n\t\t' redim the array if necessary\n\t\tif m_counter mod m_length = 0 then\n\t\t\t'redimension\n\t\t\tredim Preserve m_array(m_counter + m_length - 1)\n\n\t\t\t'double the size of the array next time\n\t\t\tm_length = m_length * 2\n\t\tend if\n\tend sub\n\n\t'**\n\t'* Return the concatenated string.\n\tpublic property get value\n\t\tvalue = join(m_array, \"\")\n\tend property\n\n\t'**\n\t'* Reset the string to an empty string.\n\tpublic function clear()\n\t\tm_counter = 0\n\n\t\tRedim m_array(m_initial_length - 1)\n\t\tm_length = m_initial_length\n\tend function\nend class\n%>\n",
      "data_access": [],
      "sql_dialect": [],
      "session_state": []

      "source": "<%\n' Simple functions to convert the first 256 characters\n' of the Windows character set from and to UTF-8.\n\n' Written by Hans Kalle for Fisz\n' http://www.fisz.nl\n' http://www.codetoad.com/asp_utf8.asp\n\n'IsValidUTF8\n'  Tells if the string is valid UTF-8 encoded\n'Returns:\n'  true (valid UTF-8)\n'  false (invalid UTF-8 or not UTF-8 encoded string)\nfunction IsValidUTF8(s)\n  dim i\n  dim c\n  dim n\n\n  IsValidUTF8 = false\n  i = 1\n  do while i <= len(s)\n    c = asc(mid(s, i, 1))\n    if c and &H80 then\n      n = 1\n      do while i + n < len(s)\n        if (asc(mid(s,i+n,1)) and &HC0) <> &H80 then\n          exit do\n        end if\n        n = n + 1\n      loop\n      select case n\n      case 1\n        exit function\n      case 2\n        if (c and &HE0) <> &HC0 then\n          exit function\n        end if\n      case 3\n        if (c and &HF0) <> &HE0 then\n          exit function\n        end if\n      case 4\n        if (c and &HF8) <> &HF0 then\n          exit function\n        end if\n      case else\n        exit function\n      end select\n      i = i + n\n    else\n      i = i + 1\n    end if\n  loop\n  IsValidUTF8 = true\nend function\n\n'DecodeUTF8\n'  Decodes a UTF-8 string to the Windows character set\n'  Non-convertable characters are replace by an upside\n'  down question mark.\n'Returns:\n'  A Windows string\nfunction DecodeUTF8(s)\n  dim i\n  dim c\n  dim n\n\n  i = 1\n  do while i <= len(s)\n    c = asc(mid(s, i, 1))\n    if c and &H80 then\n      n = 1\n      do while i + n < len(s)\n        if (asc(mid(s, i + n, 1)) and &HC0) <> &H80 then\n          exit do\n        end if\n        n = n + 1\n      loop\n      if n = 2 and ((c and &HE0) = &HC0) then\n        c = asc(mid(s, i + 1, 1)) + &H40 * (c and &H01)\n      else\n        c = 191\n      end if\n      s = left(s, i - 1) + chr(c) + mid(s, i + n)\n    end if\n    i = i + 1\n  loop\n  DecodeUTF8 = s\nend function\n\n'EncodeUTF8\n'  Encodes a Windows string in UTF-8\n'Returns:\n'  A UTF-8 encoded string\nfunction EncodeUTF8(s)\n  dim i\n  dim c\n\n  i = 1\n  do while i <= len(s)\n    c = asc(mid(s, i, 1))\n    if c >= &H80 then\n      s = left(s, i - 1) + chr(&HC2 + ((c and &H40) / &H40)) + chr(c and &HBF) + mid(s, i + 1)\n      i = i + 1\n    end if\n    i = i + 1\n  loop\n  EncodeUTF8 = s\nend function\n\n\n'EncodeURI\n'  Encodes a URL/URN/URI into a valid UTF-8 URI\n'Returns:\n'  A encoded algorithm for UTF-8 encoded URIs.\nfunction EncodeURI(s)\n  dim i, c\n  i = 1\n  do while i <= len(s)\n    c = asc(mid(s, i, 1))\n    if c >= &H80 then\n      s = left(s, i - 1) & Server.UrlEncode(chr(&HC2 + ((c and &H40) / &H40)) + chr(c and &HBF)) & mid(s, i + 1)\n      i = i + 1\n    end if\n    i = i + 1\n  loop\n  EncodeURI = s\nend function\n\n\n'http://www.15seconds.com/howto/pg001122.htm\n'Submitted by Jason Beaudoin. Adapted from VisualBasicForum\nFunction Encode_UTF8(astr)\n\t'rohtext - astr\n\t'rohtext = rohtext.replace(/\\r\\n/g, \"\\n\");\n\tDim c\n\tDim utftext\n\tutftext = \"\"\n\n\tFor n = 1 To Len(astr)\n\t\tc = AscW(Mid(astr, n, 1))\n\t\tIf c < 128 Then\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Mid(astr, n, 1)\n\t\tElseIf ((c > 127) And (c < 2048)) Then\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Chr(((c \\ 64) Or 192))\n\t\t\t'((c>>6)|192);\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Chr(((c And 63) Or 128))\n\t\t\t'((c&63)|128);}\n\t\tElse\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Chr(((c \\ 144) Or 234))\n\t\t\t'((c>>12)|224);\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Chr((((c \\ 64) And 63) Or 128))\n\t\t\t'(((c>>6)&63)|128);\n\t\t\tutftext = utftext + Chr(((c And 63) Or 128))\n\t\t\t'((c&63)|128);\n\t\tEnd If\n\tNext\n\tEncode_UTF8 = utftext\nend function\n\n%>\n\n",
